Use when the user runs /design-sync or says \"sync my design system to Claude Design\".","argumentHint":"[<project hint, e.g. \"Acme DS\">]","builtin":true},{"name":"dataviz","description":"Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. When the destination is a first-party document connector (host-designated, never self-described) that renders live charts, hand it the rows (inline, or as an uploaded data file the chart cites) rather than a rendered PNG/SVG — a picture of a chart loses hover, data inspection and per-value comments. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"update-config","description":"Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"verify","description":"Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"debug","description":"Enable debug logging for this session and help diagnose issues","argumentHint":"[issue description]","builtin":true},{"name":"code-review","description":"Review the current diff, or a PR number/branch/path target, for correctness bugs (plus reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups where the model's review recipe covers them) at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud); with no level given, it reuses the level you typed last. Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review. For ultra on a GitHub.com PR target, --post asks to post the finished review’s findings to the PR as a single comment from the user’s GitHub account (not a review; the launch dialog still confirms in interactive sessions, while non-interactive mode posts on the flag alone) and --no-post hides that option.","argumentHint":"[low|medium|high|xhigh|max|ultra] [--fix] [--comment] [<pr#>|<branch>|<path>]","aliases":["review"],"builtin":true},{"name":"simplify","description":"Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.","argumentHint":"[<target>]","builtin":true},{"name":"batch","description":"Research and plan a large-scale change, then execute it in parallel across 5–30 isolated worktree agents that each open a PR.","argumentHint":"<instruction>","builtin":true},{"name":"fewer-permission-prompts","description":"Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"doctor","description":"Health-check the user's Claude Code setup and fix issues: diagnose installation health — what the `claude doctor` terminal diagnostics cover — from local data (duplicate or leftover installs, PATH, unparseable settings files, broken or colliding agent definitions, skills whose frontmatter fails to parse); find unused skills, MCP servers, and plugins versus their context cost and disable dead weight; deduplicate local CLAUDE.md files against checked-in ones; trim checked-in CLAUDE.md files by cutting content a session could derive from the codebase (directory layouts, tech-stack lists, architecture overviews) while keeping gotchas, rationale, and non-standard conventions; migrate always-loaded CLAUDE.md guidance into lazy skills and nested CLAUDE.md files; flag slow hooks and context-heavy extensions; check the installed version is current; make auto mode the default permission mode; and pre-approve frequently denied read-only commands. Use when the user asks for a doctor run, checkup, audit, tune-up, or cleanup of their Claude Code setup or configuration.","argumentHint":"","aliases":["checkup"],"builtin":true},{"name":"loop","description":"Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace.","argumentHint":"[interval] [prompt]","aliases":["proactive"],"builtin":true},{"name":"schedule","description":"Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule.","argumentHint":"","aliases":["routines"],"builtin":true},{"name":"claude-api","description":"Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"workflow-authoring","description":"Reference for writing a Workflow tool script (script API and gotchas, resume, quality patterns, worked examples). Claude desktop app (also called the in-app browser, the browser pane, Claude's browser, or \"your own browser\"), whose tools are named mcp__Claude_Browser__* when the session runs in the desktop app and mcp__remote-devices__Claude_Browser__* when a cloud session is linked to the person's computer; before those tools are turned on there may be a single enable__mcp__remote-devices__Claude_Browser tool instead. It covers the pane's persistent sign-ins, tabs and preview_start, reading pages as text, site approvals, what the pane cannot open, and what to do when it cannot be reached. It is not for Claude in Chrome (mcp__claude-in-chrome__* tools), which has its own skill, and it does not decide which browser to use. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["built-in-browser"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:chrome-browser","description":"Read this skill before the first step that uses Claude in Chrome, the browser extension whose tools are named mcp__claude-in-chrome__* (also called Chrome, the browser extension, or the external browser) and which acts in the person's real Chrome with their own sign-ins; before those tools are turned on there may be a single enable__mcp__claude-in-chrome tool instead. It covers loading the tools in one ToolSearch call, checking the person's open tabs and working in a new tab, site permissions, GIF recordings, console logs, dialogs to avoid, and when to stop and ask. It is not for the built-in browser (mcp__Claude_Browser__* or mcp__remote-devices__Claude_Browser__* tools), which has its own skill, and it does not decide which browser to use. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["chrome-browser"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:computer-use","description":"Read this skill before the first step of any request to do something in an app on the person's own computer (Notes, Finder, System Settings, any desktop app), to look at their screen, or for \"computer use\". Computer use (desktop control) lets Claude take screenshots of the person's desktop and control it with clicks, typing and scrolling through the Claude desktop app; its tools are named mcp__computer-use__* when the session runs in the desktop app and mcp__remote-devices__computer_* when a cloud session is linked to the person's computer; before computer use is turned on for a conversation there may be no such tools, only an enable__mcp__remote-devices__computer tool, which turns it on. It covers turning it on, picking the right tool, the access flow, and the safety rules for tiered apps, links and financial actions. It is not for websites, which go through Claude in Chrome or the built-in browser and their own skills. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["computer-use"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:deep-research","description":"Use this skill when the user's prompt requires (1) researching a topic across multiple sources, comparing options or alternatives, analyzing trends or history, understanding markets or industries, or reviewing literature or studies and (2) synthesizing that research into a comprehensive, narrative report. If you're planning to search the web or internal knowledge bases, consider using this skill. This skill coordinates research subagents, so use it only when you have a tool for spawning subagents (the Agent or Task tool); otherwise, research the question directly. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":""},{"name":"anthropic-skills:docs","description":"docs (editable docs people share and comment on; the default for any document, named as a doc or not: a document, report, proposal, resume, cover letter, letter, contract, policy, form, template, worksheet, essay, handbook, guide, how-to, cheat sheet, SOP or other writing to keep, share, collaborate on, send, submit, print or sign; a doc exports to Word, PDF, Markdown or Google Docs, so needing a file to send, attach, upload, submit or print is no reason to pick Word, and a file nobody asked for is a doc, not Word; a plan, comparison, summary or notes asked in chat stays in chat; a pasted claude.ai artifact link may be a doc: check with docs tools first; Word or another file format named, tracked changes wanted, or a .docx to change or use as a template → that format's skill): making one → if no docs-connector instructions are in context, call its `guide` (topic.instructions) first; then create the doc (headings only, no body) before any search, file read or plan, even with files attached. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["docs"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:docx","description":"Use this skill whenever the user wants to create, read, edit, or manipulate Word documents (.docx) or Word templates (.dotx). Triggers include: any mention of Microsoft Word Documents, such as 'Word doc', 'word document', '.docx', '.dotx', 'microsoft doc'. Also use when extracting or reorganizing content from .docx or .dotx files, inserting or replacing images in documents, find-and-replace in Word files, working with tracked changes or comments, or converting content into a polished Word document. If the user asks for a deliverable as a Word or .docx file (to download, email or print), use this skill. However, if they ask for a document, page, report, memo, or notes WITHOUT naming a file format and the session offers Claude's own dedicated document or page skill or connector, use that instead, even if they will email or print it. Do NOT use for PDFs, spreadsheets, Google Docs, or coding unrelated to document generation. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["docx"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:google-workspace","description":"Read this before the first Google Drive, Docs, Sheets or Slides connector call whenever the task creates or changes a Google file. Use this skill whenever the user wants to create or change a Google Doc, Sheet or Slides file in their Google Drive. Triggers include: a request that names Google Docs, Sheets, Slides or Drive and asks to make, edit, format, copy or rename a file; a docs.google.com link with a request to change that file, even a one-line fix or suggested edits; and any follow-up change to a Google file from earlier in the chat, even \"change it\" or \"add a tab\". Includes helper scripts for document positions, cell ranges and slide layout. However, if the user asks for a doc, deck or spreadsheet without naming Google, or gives a Google file only as source material for something new, use Claude's own output type instead. Do NOT use for read-only questions about a Google file, or for Word, Excel, PowerPoint or PDF files. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["google-workspace"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:import-memory","description":"Import a memory export from another AI assistant into Claude's memory — conversationally, additively, and with the content treated as data. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["import-memory"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:morning","description":"Render the user's morning brief as a styled HTML artifact, or set it up as a recurring weekday task. Use only when the user explicitly asks to run, see, or set up their morning brief, or if they invoke /morning by name. A question about their day, schedule, or calendar is not by itself a request for the brief; answer it directly instead. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["morning"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:pdf","description":"Use this skill whenever the user wants to do anything with PDF files. This includes reading or extracting text/tables from PDFs, combining or merging multiple PDFs into one, splitting PDFs apart, rotating pages, adding watermarks, creating new PDFs, filling PDF forms, encrypting/decrypting PDFs, extracting images, and OCR on scanned PDFs to make them searchable. If the user mentions a .pdf file or asks to produce one, use this skill. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["pdf"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:pptx","description":"Use this skill any time a .pptx or .potx file is involved in any way — as input, output, or both. This includes: creating slide decks, pitch decks, or presentations as PowerPoint (.pptx) files; reading, parsing, or extracting text from any .pptx or .potx file (even if the extracted content will be used elsewhere, like in an email, summary, or creating a different type of slide deck); editing, modifying, or updating existing presentations; combining or splitting slide files; working with templates (.potx), layouts, speaker notes, or comments. Trigger whenever the user asks for a PowerPoint or .pptx file, or references a .pptx or .potx filename, regardless of what they plan to do with the content afterward. However, when the user asks for a deck, slides, a slide deck, or a presentation without naming a file format, default to using a dedicated slide-deck artifact type or a separate slides skill if this session offers one; otherwise, use this skill. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["pptx"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:setup-writing-style","description":"Learns how the user writes from their own sent messages and docs, and builds a voice profile so future drafts sound like them instead of generic AI. The profile is saved as the my-writing-style skill. Use when the user asks to set up, learn, or capture their writing voice, or complains that drafts sound generic or unlike them and no my-writing-style profile exists. Only for drafting text the user will send as themselves, not for Claude's own replies. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["setup-writing-style"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:skill-creator","description":"Create new skills, modify and improve existing skills, and measure skill performance. Use when users want to create a skill from scratch, edit, or optimize an existing skill, run evals to test a skill, benchmark skill performance with variance analysis, or optimize a skill's description for better triggering accuracy. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["skill-creator"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:xlsx","description":"Use this skill any time a spreadsheet file is the primary input or output. This means any task where the user wants to: open, read, edit, or fix an existing .xlsx, .xlsm, .xltx, .csv, or .tsv file (e.g., adding columns, computing formulas, formatting, charting, cleaning messy data); create a new spreadsheet from scratch or from other data sources; or convert between tabular file formats. Trigger especially when the user references a spreadsheet file by name or path — even casually (like \"the xlsx in my downloads\") — and wants something done to it or produced from it. Also trigger for cleaning or restructuring messy tabular data files (malformed rows, misplaced headers, junk data) into proper spreadsheets. The deliverable must be a spreadsheet file. Do NOT trigger when the primary deliverable is a Word document, HTML report, standalone Python script, database pipeline, or Google Sheets API integration, even if tabular data is involved. Insertion-only is itself the structural safeguard: a reviewer can verify the audited text survived by reading that the diff contains no deletions.\", \"architectural_tier\": 4, \"context\": \"Read this as the junior-engineer briefing it is. Because no line in the record says which remedy was chosen, an implementer may legitimately pick either, and picking the facade rebuilds UI for per-tenant MFA policy, IP whitelists, API keys, webhooks and domain verification, which the APA-034 Evidence at :195 and Verification at :202 establish have no enforcement implementation anywhere in the platform, on a route the same Verification shows is an orphan whose every read funnels into a fabricator. Together they prove must_satisfy key-change-0: the one allowed path was edited, its parse-anchored structure survived, and no other surface moved. must_satisfy plan_contract:plan_architectural_tier_missing is not proven by a shell command and this plan does not pretend otherwise: it is proven by `plan_content.architectural_tier` being present and equal to 4 in this body, which the kernel reads at submit and again as the `plan_contract_complete` gate row before CONVERGED. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UEvz3xf8B4w1HqrFCwLknj","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t<!-- markdownlint-disable MD011 MD013 MD029 MD033 MD034 MD037 MD038 MD049 MD052 -->\n2\t<!-- WHY: imported verbatim FE<->BE<->DB audit evidence. The quoted TypeScript is\n3\t     what makes a finding checkable, and markdown's inline rules cannot tell it\n4\t     from markup: `Record<string, T>` and `[P]['req']` read as inline HTML and a\n5\t     reference link, `(typeof X)[number]` as a reversed link, snake_case\n6\t     fragments as emphasis, a template literal as a code span with spaces, an\n7\t     internal service URL as a bare URL, and an inline \"1)\" enumeration as an\n8\t     ordered list that starts at 2. Long lines are identifier-dense finding\n9\t     titles and evidence paths that cannot wrap without breaking the reference.\n10\t     Reflowing them would corrupt the record this file exists to preserve --\n11\t     the same rationale scripts/ci/markdownlint-changed.mjs states for its\n12\t     changed-line filter. Structure is enforced by the parsers instead:\n13\t     tools/gates/finding-registry.ts and tools/gates/commit-msg-validator.ts. -->\n14\t\n15\t# Tenant Configuration & Provisioning Settings — findings\n16\t\n17\t> Part of [Admin Panel E2E Audit](../README.md). IDs `APA-xxx` are stable; severity shown is the\n18\t> verified severity where status is CONFIRMED, else the auditor's grade pending verification.\n19\t\n20\t## TenantConfigurationPage — `/admin/tenants/:tenantId/configuration` — verdict: **MOCK_ONLY**\n21\t\n22\t**Chain:** Routing and auth verified working: FE tenantConfigApi\n23\t(web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts) hits '/api/settings/tenant/:tenantId/_',\n24\tnginx rewrites ^/api/(._) to /api/v1/$1 (infrastructure/nginx/droplet.conf:382), matching\n25\tadmin-api's global prefix 'api/v1' (libs/backend-common/src/bootstrap/create-service-app.ts:610) +\n26\tTenantConfigurationController @Controller('settings/tenant')\n27\t(apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts:42); all\n28\trequests pass the global PlatformAdminGuard (RS256 JWT + SUPER_ADMIN,\n29\tapps/admin-api-service/src/app.module.ts:283-290, guards/platform-admin.guard.ts:59,155-177). But\n30\tthe persistence layer does not exist: TenantConfigurationService is an explicit 'legacy adapter' —\n31\tevery GET synthesizes the same hardcoded createDefaultTenantConfiguration() object for every tenant\n32\t(tenant-configuration.service.ts:349-358, entities/tenant-configuration.entity.ts:277-389) and every\n33\tmutation (all 9 tab saves, API keys, webhooks, domain verification, IP lists, module toggles) throws\n34\tGoneException 410 (tenant-configuration.service.ts:360-362). The backing table\n35\tadmin.tenant_configurations was dropped by migration 1801400000000 (rows archived to\n36\tadmin.retired_config_backups); the promised config-service replacement was never wired for tenant\n37\tscope — usePlatformConfiguration.ts only covers the 'platform' service namespace. The page is fully\n38\trendered, routed (Module.tsx:114) and looks functional, but is 100% decorative.\n39\t\n40\t**Endpoints exercised:** `GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/user-limits`;\n41\t`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/storage`;\n42\t`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/api`;\n43\t`POST /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/api-keys, DELETE /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/api-keys/:keyId`;\n44\t`GET/POST /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/webhooks, PUT/DELETE /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/webhooks/:webhookId`;\n45\t`GET /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/domain, POST .../domain/verify, POST .../domain/confirm`;\n46\t`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/branding`;\n47\t`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/security, POST/DELETE .../security/ip-whitelist(/:ip), POST/DELETE .../security/ip-blacklist(/:ip)`;\n48\t`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/notifications`;\n49\t`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/features, POST .../features/modules/:moduleCode/enable|disable`;\n50\t`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/data-retention`;\n51\t`POST /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/webhooks/:webhookId/test (FE only — no backend route)`\n52\t\n53\t**DB tables:**\n54\t`admin.tenant_configurations — DROPPED by migration 1801400000000-DropRetiredLegacyConfigStores.ts (no live table backs this page)`,\n55\t`admin.retired_config_backups — write-once jsonb archive of the dropped rows; never read by this page`\n56\t\n57\t### APA-033 [HIGH] Every mutation on the page returns 410 Gone — no tenant configuration can be changed at all\n58\t\n59\t- **Status:** CONFIRMED+DESIGNED (audited CRITICAL → verified HIGH)\n60\t- **Symptom:** All write endpoints route into TenantConfigurationService.throwLegacyGone(): the Save\n61\t  buttons on all 9 tabs (user limits, storage, API, branding, security, notifications, feature\n62\t  flags, data retention), API-key create/revoke, webhook create/update/delete, domain verification\n63\t  initiate/confirm, IP whitelist/blacklist add/remove, and module enable/disable ALL throw\n64\t  GoneException ('admin-api direct tenant_configurations writes are retired; use config-service\n65\t  effective configuration APIs'). The FE catches these and shows generic 'Failed to save X.'\n66\t  banners, so the operator gets no hint that the feature is permanently retired rather than\n67\t  transiently failing. The entire admin surface for per-tenant configuration is a dead end.\n68\t- **Evidence:**\n69\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts:94-95`\n70\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts:148-154`\n71\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts:208-222`\n72\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts:263-277`\n73\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts:303-309`\n74\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts:360-362`\n75\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:272-285`\n76\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:572-583`\n77\t- **Verification:** Prior adversarial verification confirmed the chain end-to-end; re-reading\n78\t  confirms it and reveals the full retirement context. Every write method in\n79\t  apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts is typed `: never`\n80\t  and terminates in throwLegacyGone() (GoneException 410); tenant-configuration.controller.ts wires\n81\t  all PUT/POST/DELETE on settings/tenant/\\* to them. The backing table admin.tenant_configurations\n82\t  was archived into admin.retired_config_backups and DROPPED by migration\n83\t  1801400000000-DropRetiredLegacyConfigStores.ts (ORPHAN-HIGH-364) — forward-only, down() is a\n84\t  deliberate no-op. The named successor ('config-service effective configuration APIs') is a generic\n85\t  tenant/service/key/environment KV exposed only as a federated GraphQL subgraph\n86\t  (configuration.resolver.ts: effectiveConfiguration, effectiveConfigurationsByService,\n87\t  setConfiguration); it has no structured tenant-configuration model and no API-key minting, webhook\n88\t  registry, or domain-verification capability. Severity stays HIGH (not CRITICAL): total loss of an\n89\t  admin capability plus fabricated GET data (defaultConfiguration() with epoch timestamps)\n90\t  misleading operators, but no data corruption or security breach — writes fail closed.\n91\t- **Root cause:** The chain was severed intentionally at the BE service layer as the backend half of\n92\t  a config-store retirement that was never finished. The DB-audit remediation (ORPHAN-HIGH-364)\n93\t  retired the 'legacy trio' (admin.global_configs, admin.system_settings,\n94\t  admin.tenant_configurations) to config-service: writes were 410'd, entities undecorated, tables\n95\t  archived+dropped. But the migration stopped at the service boundary — neither the successor\n96\t  capability (structured tenant-config sections in config-service; today it is a bare KV with no\n97\t  section vocabulary, and no replacement exists for the behavioral resources: API-key minting,\n98\t  webhooks, domain verification) nor the consumer (TenantConfigurationPage + tenantConfigApi, still\n99\t  pointed at the retired routes) was migrated. The drift stayed invisible for 'multiple releases'\n100\t  (the migration's own words) because of two structural gaps: (1) the FE api layer is a hand-written\n101\t  mirror of retired backend interfaces (tenant-config.ts literally says 'mirror backend entity\n102\t  interfaces') with no build- or test-time contract binding FE route usage to live BE routes — a\n103\t  route that throws 410-by-design is indistinguishable from a live one to the FE build; and (2) the\n104\t  GET endpoints synthesize plausible per-tenant defaults instead of failing, so the page renders\n105\t  convincingly and only breaks on Save, where the FE catch blocks discard error detail into generic\n106\t  'Failed to save X.' banners.\n107\t- **Fix design:** Systemic class: half-retired legacy surface (BE 410'd, FE untouched, successor\n108\t  unbuilt) — the identical pattern exists in system-setting.service.ts:419. Fix at pattern level\n109\t  plus local application, completing the retirement in the direction the decision-of-record chose\n110\t  (config-service owns configuration).\n111\t\n112\tPATTERN LEVEL. (A) Tier-3 gate — route-liveness contract: new architecture spec that boots/reflects\n113\tthe admin-api Nest route table, statically extracts every apiFetch path from\n114\tweb/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/\\*.ts, and fails when an FE-referenced route is\n115\tunregistered OR resolves to a `never`-typed legacy-gone service method. This turns any future\n116\t'retire the backend, forget the FE' into a red PR, and immediately flags both this page, the\n117\tsystem-settings sibling, and the already-commented dead testWebhook endpoint. (B) Tier-1 contract\n118\tSSoT: move the nine section shapes (UserLimitsConfig … FeatureFlagsConfig, WebhookConfig,\n119\tTenantConfiguration) out of settings/entities/tenant-configuration.entity.ts into a shared contract\n120\tlib (new libs/tenant-config-contracts, dual-consumed like backend-common) with class-validator\n121\tschemas. Admin-api DTOs derive from it, config-service validates written values against it, and the\n122\tadmin panel imports the types — deleting the hand-written mirrors in services/api/tenant-config.ts\n123\tso FE-type drift becomes a compile error.\n124\t\n125\tLOCAL APPLICATION. (1) Give config-service the section vocabulary: register serviceId 'tenant-admin'\n126\twith keys tenant.user-limits, tenant.storage, tenant.api, tenant.branding, tenant.security,\n127\ttenant.notifications, tenant.feature-flags, tenant.data-retention, tenant.domain (JSON valueType),\n128\tvalidated in configuration-validation.service.ts against the shared schemas; seed SYSTEM_TENANT_ID\n129\trows from createDefaultTenantConfiguration() in a config-service migration so the existing\n130\ttenant→system effective-resolution supplies real defaults. The DB link\n340\t  was deliberately deleted (migration 1801400000000 dropped admin.tenant_configurations; all\n341\t  admin-api writes became 410 Gone) on the strength of a successor claim — 'config-service owns\n342\t  tenant configuration now' — but the successor was never built: config-service's public GraphQL\n343\t  contract derives tenant scope exclusively from the caller's JWT, and SUPER_ADMIN is the platform's\n344\t  only tenantless principal, so it always resolves to SYSTEM_TENANT_ID with no argument to address\n345\t  any other tenant's partition; and no tenant-config key vocabulary was ever defined or seeded in\n346\t  config.configurations. `tests/invariants/tenant-config-vocabulary.spec.ts`\n437\t- **Proof of fix:** (1) Extend\n438\t  apps/config-service/src/configuration/configuration.resolver.public-contract.spec.ts: pins the\n439\t  optional tenantId targeting arg and asserts the explicit-target path is gated by the same\n440\t  PLATFORM_ADMIN_ROLES vocabulary (fail-closed), so scope resolution cannot drift. (2) New\n441\t  apps/config-service/src/configuration/**tests**/configuration.resolver.tenant-targeting.spec.ts:\n442\t  SUPER_ADMIN with explicit tenantId reads/writes that tenant's rows; a tenant-JWT caller passing a\n443\t  foreign tenantId gets ForbiddenException; effectiveConfigurationsByService for a fresh tenant\n444\t  returns the seeded SYSTEM defaults, overridden after a tenant-targeted setConfiguration. (3) New\n445\t  invariant tests/invariants/tenant-config-vocabulary.spec.ts: binds the shared-contracts\n446\t  TENANT_CONFIG_VOCABULARY to (a) the config-service seed migration rows, (b) the FE mapping\n447\t  module's key set, (c) each entry's declared consumedBy against the config-runtime allowlist, and\n448\t  (d) greps that no admin-panel services/api module references /settings/tenant/ — any surface\n449\t  missing a key, or a resurrected REST call, fails CI. (4) New\n450\t  web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/**tests**/tenant-configuration.spec.ts: effective-row ->\n451\t  section-model -> write-builder round-trip over the vocabulary. (5) Updated\n452\t  apps/admin-api-service/src/tenant/**tests**/tenant-provisioning.service.spec.ts: provisioning\n453\t  performs no configuration write (defaults are automatic via SYSTEM fallback). Compile-time:\n454\t  deleting the controller/service/entity/DTO and FE client makes any lingering caller a build error;\n455\t  nx affected --target=test and --target=lint green.\n456\t- **Effort:** L\n457\t\n458\t### APA-036 [MEDIUM] FE echoes whole GET objects back into PUTs whose DTOs lack fields — would 400 via forbidNonWhitelisted even if writes were restored\n459\t\n460\t- **Status:** DESIGNED (brief)\n461\t- **Symptom:** saveNotificationConfig sends the full TenantNotificationConfig including 'webhooks:\n462\t  WebhookConfig[]' (the GET default includes webhooks: []), but UpdateNotificationConfigDto has no\n463\t  'webhooks' property; saveFeatureFlags sends 'planOverrides' (present in the GET default), but\n464\t  UpdateFeatureFlagsDto has no 'planOverrides'. With the global ValidationPipe (whitelist +\n465\t  forbidNonWhitelisted, backend-common bootstrap), both saves would be rejected 400 before reaching\n466\t  the handler. Also numeric inputs coerce empty input to 0 (parseInt(...) || 0) which violates\n467\t  @Min(1) constraints on most DTO fields.\n468\t- **Evidence:**\n469\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:359`\n470\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:374`\n471\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts:351-425`\n472\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts:431-491`\n473\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/entities/tenant-configuration.entity.ts:205`\n474\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/entities/tenant-configuration.entity.ts:270`\n475\t  - `libs/backend-common/src/bootstrap/create-service-app.ts:8`\n476\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:625`\n477\t- **Root cause:** The FE reuses the full read-model types as write payloads: saveNotificationConfig\n478\t  PUTs the whole TenantNotificationConfig (includes webhooks:[], present in the GET default) and\n479\t  saveFeatureFlags PUTs the whole FeatureFlagsConfig (includes planOverrides:{}). Neither webhooks\n480\t  nor planOverrides exists in UpdateNotificationConfigDto/UpdateFeatureFlagsDto, so the platform\n481\t  ValidationPipe (whitelist+forbidNonWhitelisted) rejects both with 400 before the handler. Systemic\n482\t  class: read-model === write-model shape reuse (FE-type drift + DTO-whitelist rejection).\n483\t  Secondary: parseInt(e.target.value)||0 emits 0 for cleared numeric inputs, violating @Min(1) on\n484\t  most DTO fields. Currently masked by the 410 on these write endpoints.\n485\t- **Fix design:** Pattern-level: make the write-model distinct from the read-model and derived from\n486\t  the backend Update DTOs (the writable SSoT). Narrow each tenantConfigApi.updateX param type to a\n487\t  dedicated Update* type that mirrors the backend DTO exactly (drop webhooks from the notification\n488\t  update type, planOverrides from the feature-flags update type, apiKeys/ipWhitelist etc.), and have\n489\t  the save handlers construct that narrowed object (as saveApiConfig already does with an explicit\n490\t  field pick) rather than echoing the GET response. That turns any extra field into a compile error\n491\t  (tier 1). Fix the numeric inputs to clamp to the DTO minimum (or send undefined when empty)\n492\t  instead of coercing to 0. Add a contract test asserting every FE Update* type's key set is a\n493\t  subset of the corresponding backend DTO's class-validator property set so future drift is caught\n494\t  at build time (tier 3).\n495\t- **Files to change:**\n496\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts`\n497\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx`\n498\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts`\n499\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/__tests__/tenant-config-write-shape.spec.ts`\n500\t- **Effort:** M\n501\t\n502\t### APA-037 [MEDIUM] Failed tab loads leave an infinite spinner with only a dismissible banner\n503\t\n504\t- **Status:** DESIGNED (brief)\n505\t- **Symptom:** loadTabData catches errors, shows a feedback banner, and clears sectionLoading — but\n506\t  the tab renderers return renderSectionLoading() whenever their data state is null (e.g.\n507\t  renderUserLimitsTab). After a failed load (which is guaranteed never for GETs here, but applies on\n508\t  network failure) the operator sees a permanent spinner with no retry affordance; dismissing the\n509\t  banner leaves a spinner forever.\n510\t- **Evidence:**\n511\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:255-260`\n512\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:605-609`\n513\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:615-616`\n514\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:1005`\n515\t- **Root cause:** loadTabData catches load errors, shows a dismissible banner, and clears the shared\n516\t  sectionLoading boolean — but the tab renderers (e.g. renderUserLimitsTab:\n517\t  `if (!userLimits) return renderSectionLoading()`) gate the spinner on data===null, not on\n518\t  sectionLoading. A failed load leaves the section data null, so the tab shows the loading spinner\n519\t  permanently; dismissing the banner leaves a spinner with no retry. The component conflates three\n520\t  states (loading / loaded / failed) into one nullable-data + one boolean, so 'failed' is\n521\t  indistinguishable from 'still loading'. Low likelihood today because these GETs return static\n522\t  defaults and never throw, but real on any network/401 failure.\n523\t- **Fix design:** Model each section as an explicit state machine instead of nullable-data + shared\n524\t  boolean. Reuse the existing useAsyncData hook (already used by ProvisioningSettingsPage) which\n525\t  exposes {loading,error,canRetry,retry}; render an error card with a Retry button when a section is\n526\t  in the error state, so 'failed' can never render as 'loading' (tier 2 make-correct-automatic).\n527\t  Because useAsyncData is the established admin-panel pattern, adopting it per tab removes the\n528\t  bespoke sectionLoading flag entirely.\n529\t- **Files to change:**\n530\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx`\n531\t- **Effort:** M\n532\t\n533\t### APA-038 [MEDIUM] IP list UI invites CIDR ranges that the backend DTO rejects\n534\t\n535\t- **Status:** DESIGNED (brief)\n536\t- **Symptom:** The IpListManager placeholder is '192.168.1.0/24' and the copy says 'IP\n537\t  addresses/ranges', but IpAddressDto validates with @IsIP() which rejects CIDR notation — any range\n538\t  entry would 400 (contract drift; currently masked by the 410 on the same endpoints, since\n539\t  validation pipes run before the handler the user actually sees the 400 for CIDR and 410 for plain\n540\t  IPs — two different failure modes for the same button).\n541\t- **Evidence:**\n542\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:125`\n543\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:1316-1337`\n544\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts:342-345`\n545\t- **Root cause:** The single-IP add path validates with IpAddressDto.ip = @IsIP() (accepts only bare\n546\t  IPv4/IPv6, rejects CIDR), yet the IpListManager placeholder is '192.168.1.0/24' and both list\n547\t  descriptions say 'IP addresses/ranges'. Worse, the bulk security PUT validates\n548\t  ipWhitelist/ipBlacklist as @IsString({each}) — arbitrary strings, CIDR included. So the same\n549\t  domain concept ('allowed/blocked address or range') has two contradictory validators, and the UI\n550\t  invites input that only the bulk path accepts. A CIDR entry via Add 400s (@IsIP) while a bare IP\n551\t  410s — two failure modes for one button.\n552\t- **Fix design:** Encode the real domain rule once as a custom class-validator constraint\n553\t  @IsIpOrCidr() and apply it to BOTH IpAddressDto.ip and the each-item validation of\n554\t  ipWhitelist/ipBlacklist in UpdateTenantSecurityDto, replacing the mismatched @IsIP/@IsString pair\n555\t  (tier 1: the type system now expresses exactly what the UI offers, consistently across single-add\n556\t  and bulk). Add a unit spec covering bare IPv4, IPv6, valid CIDR (accepted) and garbage (rejected).\n557\t  If downstream enforcement genuinely cannot honor CIDR, the alternative root-cause fix is to strip\n558\t  'ranges' from the copy and set a bare-IP placeholder — but the 'ranges' intent plus the\n559\t  CIDR-accepting bulk path indicate CIDR should be supported.\n560\t- **Files to change:**\n561\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts`\n562\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/validators/is-ip-or-cidr.validator.ts`\n563\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/__tests__/is-ip-or-cidr.validator.spec.ts`\n564\t- **Effort:** S\n565\t\n566\t### APA-039 [LOW] Unvalidated bodies on several endpoints: controller uses service interfaces as DTOs\n567\t\n568\t- **Status:** DESIGNED (brief)\n569\t- **Symptom:** createApiKey, createWebhook, domain verify, and updateBranding take types imported\n570\t  from the service file (plain TS interfaces, not class-validator classes), so the global\n571\t  ValidationPipe skips them entirely (non-class metatype). Additionally the FE createTenantApiKey\n572\t  payload omits 'createdBy' which the CreateApiKeyDto interface requires — invisible at runtime\n573\t  because nothing validates it. Moot while the endpoints 410, but a latent validation gap if\n574\t  restored.\n575\t- **Evidence:**\n576\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts:18-26`\n577\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts:46-51`\n578\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts:263-271`\n579\t- **Root cause:** createConfiguration, updateConfiguration, createApiKey, createWebhook,\n580\t  initiateCustomDomainVerification and updateBranding type their @Body() as\n581\t  CreateTenantConfigurationDto/UpdateTenantConfigurationDto/CreateApiKeyDto/CreateWebhookDto/VerifyDomainDto/UpdateBrandingDto\n582\t  — all plain `export interface`s in tenant-configuration.service.ts. Interfaces erase at compile\n583\t  time, so the param metatype is Object and the global ValidationPipe's shouldValidate returns\n584\t  false: these bodies are completely unvalidated (no whitelist, no type checks). Systemic class:\n585\t  unvalidated interface-DTO. Also FE createTenantApiKey omits createdBy that the CreateApiKeyDto\n586\t  interface requires — invisible because nothing validates it (and createdBy should come from the\n587\t  JWT, not the body). Moot while endpoints 410, latent if restored.\n588\t- **Fix design:** Convert these six interfaces into class-validator DTO classes living in\n589\t  settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts (their correct home), decorate each field, and import\n590\t  them in the controller so validation becomes automatic (tier 1). Source createApiKey's createdBy\n591\t  from the JWT via getAuthUserId (matching the C6 pattern already applied to the PUT handlers)\n592\t  rather than the request body, and drop createdBy from the FE payload. Make the whole class\n593\t  detectable: add an architecture spec that reflects over every admin-api @Body() param and asserts\n594\t  its metatype is a class carrying class-validator metadata (never an interface/Object), so an\n595\t  interface-as-DTO regression fails CI (tier 3).\n596\t- **Files to change:**\n597\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts`\n598\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts`\n599\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts`\n600\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts`\n601\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/__tests__/body-dto-is-class.architecture.spec.ts`\n602\t- **Effort:** M\n603\t\n604\t### APA-040 [LOW] testWebhook FE method targets a route that does not exist\n605\t\n606\t- **Status:** DESIGNED (brief)\n607\t- **Symptom:** tenantConfigApi.testWebhook POSTs to /settings/tenant/:id/webhooks/:id/test; the\n608\t  controller has no such route (would 404). The FE file itself admits 'no backend endpoint yet'. Not\n609\t  reachable from this page's UI, but exported and re-exported via settingsApi.\n610\t- **Evidence:**\n611\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts:323-328`\n612\t  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts:214-243`\n613\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/settings.ts:57`\n614\t- **Root cause:** tenantConfigApi.testWebhook POSTs /settings/tenant/:id/webhooks/:id/test, but\n615\t  TenantConfigurationController exposes no such route (GET/POST webhooks, PUT/DELETE\n616\t  webhooks/:webhookId only) — the call 404s. The FE comment itself admits 'no backend endpoint yet'.\n617\t  It is unused by TenantConfigurationPage but exported and re-exported through\n618\t  settingsApi.testWebhook. Systemic class: FE API method with no backend route\n619\t  (FE-route-with-no-backend).\n620\t- **Fix design:** Remove the dead testWebhook method from tenant-config.ts and its re-export in\n621\t  settings.ts — shipping a client method for a nonexistent endpoint is the defect. If webhook\n622\t  test-fire is a wanted capability, it must be built backend-first (controller route + service +\n623\t  DTO) and only then wired to a FE method. To catch this class going forward, add a route-parity\n624\t  test that extracts every apiFetch path literal in the admin-panel services and asserts each\n625\t  resolves to a declared admin-api route (tier 3 detectable).\n626\t- **Files to change:**\n627\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts`\n628\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/settings.ts`\n629\t  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/__tests__/route-parity.spec.ts`\n630\t- **Effort:** S\n631\t\n632\t## ProvisioningSettingsPage — `/admin/settings/provisioning` — verdict: **BROKEN**\n633\t\n634\t**Chain:** FE systemSettingsApi.getProvisioningConfig/updateProvisioningConfig\n635\t(web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/settings.ts:123-126) hit GET/PUT\n636\t'/api/system/settings/provisioning-config'; nginx rewrites to /api/v1/... which matches\n637\tGlobalSettingsController @Controller('system/settings') @Get/@Put('provisioning-config')\n638\t(apps/admin-api-service/src/system-management/controllers/global-settings.controller.ts:676-692),\n639\tguarded by the global PlatformAdminGuard (SEC-M19 removed @Public()). The GET reads NO database —\n640\tGlobalSettingsService.getProvisioningConfig() returns PROVISIONING\\__ env vars with hardcoded\n641\tfallbacks (global-settings.service.ts:879-895,930-944). The PUT unconditionally throws GoneException\n642\t410 (global-settings.service.ts:900-907), so the page's Save button can never succeed;\n643\tadmin.global_configs was dropped by migration 1801400000000 and config-service holds no\n644\tprovisioning._ keys (no replacement write path exists). The sole downstream consumer (sensor-service\n645\tInstallerScriptService) cannot even reach the GET (wrong base URL, missing /api/v1 prefix,\n646\tnon-SUPER_ADMIN bearer) and silently falls back to its own env vars — so nothing this page displays\n647\tor would save affects any real behavior.\n648\t\n649\t**Endpoints exercised:** `GET /api/v1/system/settings/provisioning-config`;\n650\t`PUT /api/v1/system/settings/provisioning-config (always 410 Gone)`\n651\t\n652\t**DB tables:**\n653\t`none — GET is process-env-backed; admin.global_configs DROPPED by migration 1801400000000-DropRetiredLegacyConfigStores.ts; config-service has no provisioning.* keys`\n654\t\n655\t### APA-041 [HIGH] Save always fails: PUT provisioning-config unconditionally throws 410 Gone and no replacement write path exists\n656\t\n657\t- **Status:** CONFIRMED+DESIGNED (audited CRITICAL → verified HIGH)\n658\t- **Symptom:** GlobalSettingsService.updateProvisioningConfig() is 'never'-typed and throws\n659\t  GoneException ('admin-api direct global_configs writes are retired; use config-service effective\n660\t  configuration APIs'). The page's entire purpose is editing these six fields, so its primary flow\n661\t  cannot succeed for any input. Unlike the SystemSettingsPage (migrated to config-service GraphQL"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"bec7fa7d-94f6-4201-aaad-b5522e672063","uuid":"91db8581-d9a7-4661-8d0b-6a50d867e16e","timestamp":"2026-10-10T12:35:30.165Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-implementer-45033f9cd83a/docs/reviews/claude/2026-07-20-admin-panel-e2e-audit/findings/tenant-config.md","content":"<!-- markdownlint-disable MD011 MD013 MD029 MD033 MD034 MD037 MD038 MD049 MD052 -->\n<!-- WHY: imported verbatim FE<->BE<->DB audit evidence. The quoted TypeScript is\n     what makes a finding checkable, and markdown's inline rules cannot tell it\n     from markup: `Record<string, T>` and `[P]['req']` read as inline HTML and a\n     reference link, `(typeof X)[number]` as a reversed link, snake_case\n     fragments as emphasis, a template literal as a code span with spaces, an\n     internal service URL as a bare URL, and an inline \"1)\" enumeration as an\n     ordered list that starts at 2. Long lines are identifier-dense finding\n     titles and evidence paths that cannot wrap without breaking the reference.\n     Reflowing them would corrupt the record this file exists to preserve --\n     the same rationale scripts/ci/markdownlint-changed.mjs states for its\n     changed-line filter. Structure is enforced by the parsers instead:\n     tools/gates/finding-registry.ts and tools/gates/commit-msg-validator.ts. -->\n\n# Tenant Configuration & Provisioning Settings — findings\n\n> Part of [Admin Panel E2E Audit](../README.md). IDs `APA-xxx` are stable; severity shown is the\n> verified severity where status is CONFIRMED, else the auditor's grade pending verification.\n\n## TenantConfigurationPage — `/admin/tenants/:tenantId/configuration` — verdict: **MOCK_ONLY**\n\n**Chain:** Routing and auth verified working: FE tenantConfigApi\n(web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts) hits '/api/settings/tenant/:tenantId/_',\nnginx rewrites ^/api/(._) to /api/v1/$1 (infrastructure/nginx/droplet.conf:382), matching\nadmin-api's global prefix 'api/v1' (libs/backend-common/src/bootstrap/create-service-app.ts:610) +\nTenantConfigurationController @Controller('settings/tenant')\n(apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts:42); all\nrequests pass the global PlatformAdminGuard (RS256 JWT + SUPER_ADMIN,\napps/admin-api-service/src/app.module.ts:283-290, guards/platform-admin.guard.ts:59,155-177). But\nthe persistence layer does not exist: TenantConfigurationService is an explicit 'legacy adapter' —\nevery GET synthesizes the same hardcoded createDefaultTenantConfiguration() object for every tenant\n(tenant-configuration.service.ts:349-358, entities/tenant-configuration.entity.ts:277-389) and every\nmutation (all 9 tab saves, API keys, webhooks, domain verification, IP lists, module toggles) throws\nGoneException 410 (tenant-configuration.service.ts:360-362). The backing table\nadmin.tenant_configurations was dropped by migration 1801400000000 (rows archived to\nadmin.retired_config_backups); the promised config-service replacement was never wired for tenant\nscope — usePlatformConfiguration.ts only covers the 'platform' service namespace. The page is fully\nrendered, routed (Module.tsx:114) and looks functional, but is 100% decorative.\n\n**Endpoints exercised:** `GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/user-limits`;\n`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/storage`;\n`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/api`;\n`POST /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/api-keys, DELETE /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/api-keys/:keyId`;\n`GET/POST /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/webhooks, PUT/DELETE /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/webhooks/:webhookId`;\n`GET /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/domain, POST .../domain/verify, POST .../domain/confirm`;\n`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/branding`;\n`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/security, POST/DELETE .../security/ip-whitelist(/:ip), POST/DELETE .../security/ip-blacklist(/:ip)`;\n`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/notifications`;\n`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/features, POST .../features/modules/:moduleCode/enable|disable`;\n`GET/PUT /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/data-retention`;\n`POST /api/v1/settings/tenant/:tenantId/webhooks/:webhookId/test (FE only — no backend route)`\n\n**DB tables:**\n`admin.tenant_configurations — DROPPED by migration 1801400000000-DropRetiredLegacyConfigStores.ts (no live table backs this page)`,\n`admin.retired_config_backups — write-once jsonb archive of the dropped rows; never read by this page`\n\n### APA-033 [HIGH] Every mutation on the page returns 410 Gone — no tenant configuration can be changed at all\n\n- **Status:** CONFIRMED+DESIGNED (audited CRITICAL → verified HIGH)\n- **Symptom:** All write endpoints route into TenantConfigurationService.throwLegacyGone(): the Save\n  buttons on all 9 tabs (user limits, storage, API, branding, security, notifications, feature\n  flags, data retention), API-key create/revoke, webhook create/update/delete, domain verification\n  initiate/confirm, IP whitelist/blacklist add/remove, and module enable/disable ALL throw\n  GoneException ('admin-api direct tenant_configurations writes are retired; use config-service\n  effective configuration APIs'). The FE catches these and shows generic 'Failed to save X.'\n  banners, so the operator gets no hint that the feature is permanently retired rather than\n  transiently failing. Every write method in\n  apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts is typed `: never`\n  and terminates in throwLegacyGone() (GoneException 410); tenant-configuration.controller.ts wires\n  all PUT/POST/DELETE on settings/tenant/\\* to them. The backing table admin.tenant_configurations\n  was archived into admin.retired_config_backups and DROPPED by migration\n  1801400000000-DropRetiredLegacyConfigStores.ts (ORPHAN-HIGH-364) — forward-only, down() is a\n  deliberate no-op. The named successor ('config-service effective configuration APIs') is a generic\n  tenant/service/key/environment KV exposed only as a federated GraphQL subgraph\n  (configuration.resolver.ts: effectiveConfiguration, effectiveConfigurationsByService,\n  setConfiguration); it has no structured tenant-configuration model and no API-key minting, webhook\n  registry, or domain-verification capability. Severity stays HIGH (not CRITICAL): total loss of an\n  admin capability plus fabricated GET data (defaultConfiguration() with epoch timestamps)\n  misleading operators, but no data corruption or security breach — writes fail closed.\n- **Root cause:** The chain was severed intentionally at the BE service layer as the backend half of\n  a config-store retirement that was never finished. The DB-audit remediation (ORPHAN-HIGH-364)\n  retired the 'legacy trio' (admin.global_configs, admin.system_settings,\n  admin.tenant_configurations) to config-service: writes were 410'd, entities undecorated, tables\n  archived+dropped. But the migration stopped at the service boundary — neither the successor\n  capability (structured tenant-config sections in config-service; today it is a bare KV with no\n  section vocabulary, and no replacement exists for the behavioral resources: API-key minting,\n  webhooks, domain verification) nor the consumer (TenantConfigurationPage + tenantConfigApi, still\n  pointed at the retired routes) was migrated. The drift stayed invisible for 'multiple releases'\n  (the migration's own words) because of two structural gaps: (1) the FE api layer is a hand-written\n  mirror of retired backend interfaces (tenant-config.ts literally says 'mirror backend entity\n  interfaces') with no build- or test-time contract binding FE route usage to live BE routes — a\n  route that throws 410-by-design is indistinguishable from a live one to the FE build; and (2) the\n  GET endpoints synthesize plausible per-tenant defaults instead of failing, so the page renders\n  convincingly and only breaks on Save, where the FE catch blocks discard error detail into generic\n  'Failed to save X.' banners.\n- **Fix design:** Systemic class: half-retired legacy surface (BE 410'd, FE untouched, successor\n  unbuilt) — the identical pattern exists in system-setting.service.ts:419. This turns any future\n'retire the backend, forget the FE' into a red PR, and immediately flags both this page, the\nsystem-settings sibling, and the already-commented dead testWebhook endpoint. WHY it drifted: the retirement (ORPHAN-HIGH-364) and the replacement\n  (ORPHAN-HIGH-373) were tracked as separate findings, and 373 was closed after delivering only the\n  platform namespace (SystemSettingsPage). Their dead 410-calling UI is removed from the\npage in this fix, and each is opened as its own tracked finding with owner + deadline + ID, listed\nin the PR per CLAUDE.md debt rules. (7) Downstream consumption becomes possible and auditable:\nextend ConfigRuntimeGetRequest in libs/event-contracts/src/config-runtime.ts with tenant scope\nresolved through the same effective merge, and cross-check each vocabulary entry's consumedBy\ndeclaration against the runtime per-caller allowlist in the new invariant — the 'config nobody\nreads' class becomes build-time detectable instead of silently accumulating.\n\n- **Files to change:**\n  - `libs/shared-contracts/src/tenant-configuration.ts`\n  - `libs/shared-contracts/src/index.ts`\n  - `tests/invariants/shared-contracts-no-enum-drift.spec.ts`\n  - `apps/config-service/src/configuration/configuration.resolver.ts`\n  - `apps/config-service/src/configuration/configuration.resolver.public-contract.spec.ts`\n  - `apps/config-service/src/configuration/__tests__/configuration.resolver.tenant-targeting.spec.ts`\n  - `apps/config-service/src/database/migrations/1801500000000-SeedTenantSettingsDefaults.ts`\n  - `libs/event-contracts/src/config-runtime.ts`\n  - `apps/config-service/src/configuration/handlers/config-runtime-nats.handler.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/tsconfig.json`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/vite.config.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/graphql/tenant-configuration-operations.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-configuration.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/__tests__/tenant-configuration.spec.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/hooks/useTenantConfiguration.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/hooks/adminQueryKeys.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/types/settings.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/entities/tenant-configuration.entity.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/settings.module.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/index.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/index.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/tenant/services/tenant-provisioning.service.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/tenant/__tests__/tenant-provisioning.service.spec.ts`\n  - `tests/invariants/tenant-config-vocabulary.spec.ts`\n- **Proof of fix:** (1) Extend\n  apps/config-service/src/configuration/configuration.resolver.public-contract.spec.ts: pins the\n  optional tenantId targeting arg and asserts the explicit-target path is gated by the same\n  PLATFORM_ADMIN_ROLES vocabulary (fail-closed), so scope resolution cannot drift. (2) New\n  apps/config-service/src/configuration/**tests**/configuration.resolver.tenant-targeting.spec.ts:\n  SUPER_ADMIN with explicit tenantId reads/writes that tenant's rows; a tenant-JWT caller passing a\n  foreign tenantId gets ForbiddenException; effectiveConfigurationsByService for a fresh tenant\n  returns the seeded SYSTEM defaults, overridden after a tenant-targeted setConfiguration. (3) New\n  invariant tests/invariants/tenant-config-vocabulary.spec.ts: binds the shared-contracts\n  TENANT_CONFIG_VOCABULARY to (a) the config-service seed migration rows, (b) the FE mapping\n  module's key set, (c) each entry's declared consumedBy against the config-runtime allowlist, and\n  (d) greps that no admin-panel services/api module references /settings/tenant/ — any surface\n  missing a key, or a resurrected REST call, fails CI. (4) New\n  web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/**tests**/tenant-configuration.spec.ts: effective-row ->\n  section-model -> write-builder round-trip over the vocabulary. (5) Updated\n  apps/admin-api-service/src/tenant/**tests**/tenant-provisioning.service.spec.ts: provisioning\n  performs no configuration write (defaults are automatic via SYSTEM fallback). Compile-time:\n  deleting the controller/service/entity/DTO and FE client makes any lingering caller a build error;\n  nx affected --target=test and --target=lint green.\n- **Effort:** L\n\n### APA-036 [MEDIUM] FE echoes whole GET objects back into PUTs whose DTOs lack fields — would 400 via forbidNonWhitelisted even if writes were restored\n\n- **Status:** DESIGNED (brief)\n- **Symptom:** saveNotificationConfig sends the full TenantNotificationConfig including 'webhooks:\n  WebhookConfig[]' (the GET default includes webhooks: []), but UpdateNotificationConfigDto has no\n  'webhooks' property; saveFeatureFlags sends 'planOverrides' (present in the GET default), but\n  UpdateFeatureFlagsDto has no 'planOverrides'. With the global ValidationPipe (whitelist +\n  forbidNonWhitelisted, backend-common bootstrap), both saves would be rejected 400 before reaching\n  the handler. Also numeric inputs coerce empty input to 0 (parseInt(...) || 0) which violates\n  @Min(1) constraints on most DTO fields.\n- **Evidence:**\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:359`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:374`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts:351-425`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts:431-491`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/entities/tenant-configuration.entity.ts:205`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/entities/tenant-configuration.entity.ts:270`\n  - `libs/backend-common/src/bootstrap/create-service-app.ts:8`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:625`\n- **Root cause:** The FE reuses the full read-model types as write payloads: saveNotificationConfig\n  PUTs the whole TenantNotificationConfig (includes webhooks:[], present in the GET default) and\n  saveFeatureFlags PUTs the whole FeatureFlagsConfig (includes planOverrides:{}). Neither webhooks\n  nor planOverrides exists in UpdateNotificationConfigDto/UpdateFeatureFlagsDto, so the platform\n  ValidationPipe (whitelist+forbidNonWhitelisted) rejects both with 400 before the handler. Systemic\n  class: read-model === write-model shape reuse (FE-type drift + DTO-whitelist rejection).\n  Secondary: parseInt(e.target.value)||0 emits 0 for cleared numeric inputs, violating @Min(1) on\n  most DTO fields. Currently masked by the 410 on these write endpoints.\n- **Fix design:** Pattern-level: make the write-model distinct from the read-model and derived from\n  the backend Update DTOs (the writable SSoT). Narrow each tenantConfigApi.updateX param type to a\n  dedicated Update* type that mirrors the backend DTO exactly (drop webhooks from the notification\n  update type, planOverrides from the feature-flags update type, apiKeys/ipWhitelist etc.), and have\n  the save handlers construct that narrowed object (as saveApiConfig already does with an explicit\n  field pick) rather than echoing the GET response. That turns any extra field into a compile error\n  (tier 1). Fix the numeric inputs to clamp to the DTO minimum (or send undefined when empty)\n  instead of coercing to 0. Add a contract test asserting every FE Update* type's key set is a\n  subset of the corresponding backend DTO's class-validator property set so future drift is caught\n  at build time (tier 3).\n- **Files to change:**\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/__tests__/tenant-config-write-shape.spec.ts`\n- **Effort:** M\n\n### APA-037 [MEDIUM] Failed tab loads leave an infinite spinner with only a dismissible banner\n\n- **Status:** DESIGNED (brief)\n- **Symptom:** loadTabData catches errors, shows a feedback banner, and clears sectionLoading — but\n  the tab renderers return renderSectionLoading() whenever their data state is null (e.g.\n  renderUserLimitsTab). After a failed load (which is guaranteed never for GETs here, but applies on\n  network failure) the operator sees a permanent spinner with no retry affordance; dismissing the\n  banner leaves a spinner forever.\n- **Evidence:**\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:255-260`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:605-609`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:615-616`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:1005`\n- **Root cause:** loadTabData catches load errors, shows a dismissible banner, and clears the shared\n  sectionLoading boolean — but the tab renderers (e.g. renderUserLimitsTab:\n  `if (!userLimits) return renderSectionLoading()`) gate the spinner on data===null, not on\n  sectionLoading. A failed load leaves the section data null, so the tab shows the loading spinner\n  permanently; dismissing the banner leaves a spinner with no retry. The component conflates three\n  states (loading / loaded / failed) into one nullable-data + one boolean, so 'failed' is\n  indistinguishable from 'still loading'. Low likelihood today because these GETs return static\n  defaults and never throw, but real on any network/401 failure.\n- **Fix design:** Model each section as an explicit state machine instead of nullable-data + shared\n  boolean. Reuse the existing useAsyncData hook (already used by ProvisioningSettingsPage) which\n  exposes {loading,error,canRetry,retry}; render an error card with a Retry button when a section is\n  in the error state, so 'failed' can never render as 'loading' (tier 2 make-correct-automatic).\n  Because useAsyncData is the established admin-panel pattern, adopting it per tab removes the\n  bespoke sectionLoading flag entirely.\n- **Files to change:**\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx`\n- **Effort:** M\n\n### APA-038 [MEDIUM] IP list UI invites CIDR ranges that the backend DTO rejects\n\n- **Status:** DESIGNED (brief)\n- **Symptom:** The IpListManager placeholder is '192.168.1.0/24' and the copy says 'IP\n  addresses/ranges', but IpAddressDto validates with @IsIP() which rejects CIDR notation — any range\n  entry would 400 (contract drift; currently masked by the 410 on the same endpoints, since\n  validation pipes run before the handler the user actually sees the 400 for CIDR and 410 for plain\n  IPs — two different failure modes for the same button).\n- **Evidence:**\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:125`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/pages/TenantConfigurationPage.tsx:1316-1337`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts:342-345`\n- **Root cause:** The single-IP add path validates with IpAddressDto.ip = @IsIP() (accepts only bare\n  IPv4/IPv6, rejects CIDR), yet the IpListManager placeholder is '192.168.1.0/24' and both list\n  descriptions say 'IP addresses/ranges'. Worse, the bulk security PUT validates\n  ipWhitelist/ipBlacklist as @IsString({each}) — arbitrary strings, CIDR included. So the same\n  domain concept ('allowed/blocked address or range') has two contradictory validators, and the UI\n  invites input that only the bulk path accepts. A CIDR entry via Add 400s (@IsIP) while a bare IP\n  410s — two failure modes for one button.\n- **Fix design:** Encode the real domain rule once as a custom class-validator constraint\n  @IsIpOrCidr() and apply it to BOTH IpAddressDto.ip and the each-item validation of\n  ipWhitelist/ipBlacklist in UpdateTenantSecurityDto, replacing the mismatched @IsIP/@IsString pair\n  (tier 1: the type system now expresses exactly what the UI offers, consistently across single-add\n  and bulk). Add a unit spec covering bare IPv4, IPv6, valid CIDR (accepted) and garbage (rejected).\n  If downstream enforcement genuinely cannot honor CIDR, the alternative root-cause fix is to strip\n  'ranges' from the copy and set a bare-IP placeholder — but the 'ranges' intent plus the\n  CIDR-accepting bulk path indicate CIDR should be supported.\n- **Files to change:**\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/validators/is-ip-or-cidr.validator.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/__tests__/is-ip-or-cidr.validator.spec.ts`\n- **Effort:** S\n\n### APA-039 [LOW] Unvalidated bodies on several endpoints: controller uses service interfaces as DTOs\n\n- **Status:** DESIGNED (brief)\n- **Symptom:** createApiKey, createWebhook, domain verify, and updateBranding take types imported\n  from the service file (plain TS interfaces, not class-validator classes), so the global\n  ValidationPipe skips them entirely (non-class metatype). Additionally the FE createTenantApiKey\n  payload omits 'createdBy' which the CreateApiKeyDto interface requires — invisible at runtime\n  because nothing validates it. Moot while the endpoints 410, but a latent validation gap if\n  restored.\n- **Evidence:**\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts:18-26`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts:46-51`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts:263-271`\n- **Root cause:** createConfiguration, updateConfiguration, createApiKey, createWebhook,\n  initiateCustomDomainVerification and updateBranding type their @Body() as\n  CreateTenantConfigurationDto/UpdateTenantConfigurationDto/CreateApiKeyDto/CreateWebhookDto/VerifyDomainDto/UpdateBrandingDto\n  — all plain `export interface`s in tenant-configuration.service.ts. Interfaces erase at compile\n  time, so the param metatype is Object and the global ValidationPipe's shouldValidate returns\n  false: these bodies are completely unvalidated (no whitelist, no type checks). Systemic class:\n  unvalidated interface-DTO. Also FE createTenantApiKey omits createdBy that the CreateApiKeyDto\n  interface requires — invisible because nothing validates it (and createdBy should come from the\n  JWT, not the body). Moot while endpoints 410, latent if restored.\n- **Fix design:** Convert these six interfaces into class-validator DTO classes living in\n  settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts (their correct home), decorate each field, and import\n  them in the controller so validation becomes automatic (tier 1). Source createApiKey's createdBy\n  from the JWT via getAuthUserId (matching the C6 pattern already applied to the PUT handlers)\n  rather than the request body, and drop createdBy from the FE payload. Make the whole class\n  detectable: add an architecture spec that reflects over every admin-api @Body() param and asserts\n  its metatype is a class carrying class-validator metadata (never an interface/Object), so an\n  interface-as-DTO regression fails CI (tier 3).\n- **Files to change:**\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/dto/tenant-configuration.dto.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/services/tenant-configuration.service.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/__tests__/body-dto-is-class.architecture.spec.ts`\n- **Effort:** M\n\n### APA-040 [LOW] testWebhook FE method targets a route that does not exist\n\n- **Status:** DESIGNED (brief)\n- **Symptom:** tenantConfigApi.testWebhook POSTs to /settings/tenant/:id/webhooks/:id/test; the\n  controller has no such route (would 404). The FE file itself admits 'no backend endpoint yet'. Not\n  reachable from this page's UI, but exported and re-exported via settingsApi.\n- **Evidence:**\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts:323-328`\n  - `apps/admin-api-service/src/settings/controllers/tenant-configuration.controller.ts:214-243`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/settings.ts:57`\n- **Root cause:** tenantConfigApi.testWebhook POSTs /settings/tenant/:id/webhooks/:id/test, but\n  TenantConfigurationController exposes no such route (GET/POST webhooks, PUT/DELETE\n  webhooks/:webhookId only) — the call 404s. The FE comment itself admits 'no backend endpoint yet'.\n  It is unused by TenantConfigurationPage but exported and re-exported through\n  settingsApi.testWebhook. Systemic class: FE API method with no backend route\n  (FE-route-with-no-backend).\n- **Fix design:** Remove the dead testWebhook method from tenant-config.ts and its re-export in\n  settings.ts — shipping a client method for a nonexistent endpoint is the defect. If webhook\n  test-fire is a wanted capability, it must be built backend-first (controller route + service +\n  DTO) and only then wired to a FE method. To catch this class going forward, add a route-parity\n  test that extracts every apiFetch path literal in the admin-panel services and asserts each\n  resolves to a declared admin-api route (tier 3 detectable).\n- **Files to change:**\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/tenant-config.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/settings.ts`\n  - `web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/__tests__/route-parity.spec.ts`\n- **Effort:** S\n\n## ProvisioningSettingsPage — `/admin/settings/provisioning` — verdict: **BROKEN**\n\n**Chain:** FE systemSettingsApi.getProvisioningConfig/updateProvisioningConfig\n(web/modules/admin-panel/src/services/api/settings.ts:123-126) hit GET/PUT\n'/api/system/settings/provisioning-config'; nginx rewrites to /api/v1/... which matches\nGlobalSettingsController @Controller('system/settings') @Get/@Put('provisioning-config')\n(apps/admin-api-service/src/system-management/controllers/global-settings.controller.ts:676-692),\nguarded by the global PlatformAdminGuard (SEC-M19 removed @Public()). The GET reads NO database —\nGlobalSettingsService.getProvisioningConfig() returns PROVISIONING\\__ env vars with hardcoded\nfallbacks (global-settings.service.ts:879-895,930-944). The PUT unconditionally throws GoneException\n410 (global-settings.service.ts:900-907), so the page's Save button can never succeed;\nadmin.global_configs was dropped by migration 1801400000000 and config-service holds no\nprovisioning._ keys (no replacement write path exists). The sole downstream consumer (sensor-service\nInstallerScriptService) cannot even reach the GET (wrong base URL, missing /api/v1 prefix,\nnon-SUPER_ADMIN bearer) and silently falls back to its own env vars — so nothing this page displays\nor would save affects any real behavior.\n\n**Endpoints exercised:** `GET /api/v1/system/settings/provisioning-config`;\n`PUT /api/v1/system/settings/provisioning-config (always 410 Gone)`\n\n**DB tables:**\n`none — GET is process-env-backed; admin.global_configs DROPPED by migration 1801400000000-DropRetiredLegacyConfigStores.ts; config-service has no provisioning.* keys`\n\n### APA-041 [HIGH] Save always fails: PUT provisioning-config unconditionally throws 410 Gone and no replacement write path exists\n\n- **Status:** CONFIRMED+DESIGNED (audited CRITICAL → verified HIGH)\n- **Symptom:** GlobalSettingsService.updateProvisioningConfig() is 'never'-typed and throws\n  GoneException ('admin-api direct global_configs writes are retired; use config-service effective\n  configuration APIs'). The page's entire purpose is editing these six fields, so its primary flow\n  cannot succeed for any input. Unlike the SystemSettingsPage (migrated to config-service GraphQL","numLines":661,"startLine":1,"totalLines":1425,"truncatedByTokenCap":true}}}

The APA-033 LOCAL APPLICATION facade\nrebuild and the Files-to-change inventory it drives are superseded, and executing that inventory is\nprohibited: it would re-expose per-tenant MFA policy, IP whitelists, tenant API keys, webhooks and\ndomain verification, which have no enforcement implementation anywhere in the platform.
